======================================================================
WORLD HISTORY KNOWLEDGE BASE
======================================================================

Entry 1
Category: Ancient Civilizations
Concept: History
Definition: The study of past events based on evidence and records.
Key Points:
- Major topic in world history.
- Frequently taught in history curricula.
- Influenced global civilisation and modern society.
Example:
History is an important topic in world history.
Related Concepts:
Civilizations, Empires, Revolutions, World Wars, Global Politics

Entry 2
Category: Ancient Civilizations
Concept: Mesopotamia

- Major topic in world history.

- Influenced global civilisation and modern society.
Example:

Category: Modern World
Concept: Globalization
Definition: Increasing interconnectedness of economies and societies.
Key Points:
- Major topic in world history.
- Frequently taught in history curricula.
- Influenced global civilisation and modern society.
Example:
Globalization is an important topic in world history.
Related Concepts:
Civilizations, Empires, Revolutions, World Wars, Global Politics
